    "Is-this-a-good-customer",
    "Marketing_Campaign",
    "maternal_health_risk",
    "MIC",
    "NATICUSdroid",
    "polish_companies_bankruptcy",
    "SDSS17",
    "seismic-bumps",
    "students_dropout_and_academic_success",
    "taiwanese_bankruptcy_prediction",
]


def all_dataset_names() -> list[str]:
    """Every TabArena dataset this script runs, overlapping ones first."""
    return [overlap.tabarena_name for overlap in OVERLAP_DATASETS] + NON_OVERLAP_DATASETS


MAX_FUSION_SEQUENCES = 65535
"""TabSTAR's NumericalFusion attends over batch_size * n_tokens sequences of length 2 at once. In
training under autocast the CUDA attention kernel fails with "CUDA error: invalid argument" above
65535 of them (65535 passes, 65536 fails on an A100), which the widest TabArena datasets (over 1000
features) hit at the default batch sizes."""


def fusion_safe_batch(batch_size: int, n_tokens: int) -> int:
    """Halve `batch_size` until batch_size * n_tokens fits the fusion kernel (gradients accumulate to
    the same global batch either way, so the optimisation is unchanged)."""
    while batch_size > 1 and batch_size * n_tokens > MAX_FUSION_SEQUENCES:
        batch_size //= 2
    return batch_size


class TabSTARModel(AbstractModel):
    """TabArena wrapper around TabSTAR, exposing checkpoint selection as a config hyperparameter.

    Defined in `__main__` scope: this script runs with `debug_mode=True` (in-process backend),
    which is required for a model class defined outside an importable module.
    """

    ag_key = "TABSTAR"
    ag_name = "TabSTAR"

    pretrain_param_name = "pretrain_dataset_or_path"
    """Popped from the hyperparameters in `_fit`; forwarded to `TabSTARClassifier`/`Regressor`.

    `None` -> base checkpoint. A `BIN_`/`REG_`/`MUL_`-prefixed key -> that dataset's excluding-fold
    checkpoint. Any other string -> used verbatim as the HF repo id (for the "wrong" checkpoint).
    """

    def _fit(
        self,
        X: pd.DataFrame,
        y: pd.Series,
        num_cpus: int = 1,
        num_gpus: int = 0,
        time_limit: float | None = None,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> None:
        from tabstar.tabstar_model import TabSTARClassifier, TabSTARRegressor
        from tabstar.training.hyperparams import LORA_BATCH, VAL_BATCH

        X = self.preprocess(X, y=y, is_train=True)
        hps = dict(self._get_model_params())
        pretrain_dataset_or_path = hps.pop(self.pretrain_param_name, None)
        device = "cuda" if num_gpus > 0 else "cpu"
        is_classification = self.problem_type in ("binary", "multiclass")
        model_cls = TabSTARClassifier if is_classification else TabSTARRegressor
        n_tokens = X.shape[1] + (y.nunique() if is_classification else 1)
        # Forward AutoGluon's per-fold budget (TabArena's own TabSTAR wrapper does the same). Without
        # it a fit never self-limits, and AutoGluon aborts the whole 8-fold bag with TimeLimitExceeded
        # as soon as the folds so far project past the bag's 1 h budget (after fold 1: any fit > 450 s).
        self.model = model_cls(
            pretrain_dataset_or_path=pretrain_dataset_or_path,
            device=device,
            time_limit=time_limit,
            lora_batch=fusion_safe_batch(LORA_BATCH, n_tokens=n_tokens),
            val_batch_size=fusion_safe_batch(VAL_BATCH, n_tokens=n_tokens),
            **hps,
        )
        self.model.fit(X, y)

    def _set_default_params(self) -> None:
        pass

    @classmethod
    def supported_problem_types(cls) -> list[str]:
        return ["binary", "multiclass", "regression"]

    def _get_default_resources(self) -> tuple[int, int]:
        num_cpus = ResourceManager.get_cpu_count(only_physical_cores=True)
        num_gpus = min(1, ResourceManager.get_gpu_count_torch(cuda_only=True))
        return num_cpus, num_gpus

    def get_minimum_resources(self, is_gpu_available: bool = False) -> dict[str, int]:
        return {"num_cpus": 1, "num_gpus": 1 if is_gpu_available else 0}


class TabSTARBaseModel(TabSTARModel):
    """The base checkpoint variant, pretrained on everything."""

    ag_key = "TabSTAR_base"
    ag_name = "TabSTAR_base"


class TabSTARCorrectModel(TabSTARModel):
    """The fold checkpoint that EXCLUDED this dataset from pretraining (leakage-free)."""

    ag_key = "TabSTAR_correct"
    ag_name = "TabSTAR_correct"


class TabSTARWrongModel(TabSTARModel):
    """A different fold checkpoint that still INCLUDED this dataset (sanity control)."""

    ag_key = "TabSTAR_wrong"
    ag_name = "TabSTAR_wrong"


def build_checkpoint_generators(overlap: OverlapDataset) -> list[ConfigGenerator]:
    """The three `ConfigGenerator`s (base/correct/wrong) for one overlapping dataset.

    TabArena's bagged-experiment naming keys off `model_cls.ag_name`/`ag_key` (not
    `ConfigGenerator(name=...)`, which the naming path ignores), so each variant is its own named
    `TabSTARModel` subclass â€” a `type()`-generated class isn't picklable (AutoGluon persists the
    predictor to disk mid-fit), so these must be real module-level classes, not built dynamically
    per dataset. The checkpoint itself still varies per dataset, passed as a hyperparameter.
    """
    variants = {
        TabSTARBaseModel: None,
        TabSTARCorrectModel: overlap.tabstar_key,
        TabSTARWrongModel: PRETRAIN_FOLD_REPO_TEMPLATE.format(fold=wrong_fold(overlap.tabstar_key)),
    }
    return _generators(variants)


def build_non_overlap_generators() -> list[ConfigGenerator]:
    """Base plus the fixed fold checkpoint for a dataset outside the v1 corpus (no "wrong" arm)."""
    variants = {
        TabSTARBaseModel: None,
        TabSTARCorrectModel: PRETRAIN_FOLD_REPO_TEMPLATE.format(fold=NON_OVERLAP_FOLD),
    }
    return _generators(variants)


def _generators(variants: dict[type[TabSTARModel], str | None]) -> list[ConfigGenerator]:
    return [
        ConfigGenerator(
            model_cls=model_cls,
            manual_configs=[{TabSTARModel.pretrain_param_name: checkpoint}],
            search_space={},
        )
        for model_cls, checkpoint in variants.items()
    ]


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--tabarena_name",
        default=None,
        help="Restrict to a single TabArena dataset by its name (default: run all 51).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--skip_compare",
        action="store_true",
        help="Skip context.compare() after training (for per-dataset cluster jobs; requires "
        "only the bare tabarena package, not its [plot] extra). Aggregate later with --compare_only.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--compare_only",
        action="store_true",
        help="Skip training and only run context.compare() over already-persisted results "
        "(requires `pip install \"tabarena[plot]\"`). Mutually exclusive with --tabarena_name.",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.compare_only and args.tabarena_name is not None:
        parser.error("--compare_only runs over all overlapping datasets; drop --tabarena_name.")
    if args.compare_only and args.skip_compare:
        parser.error("--compare_only and --skip_compare are mutually exclusive.")
    return args


if __name__ == "__main__":
    args = parse_args()
    dataset_names = all_dataset_names()
    if args.tabarena_name is not None:
        if args.tabarena_name not in dataset_names:
            raise ValueError(f"{args.tabarena_name!r} not found in OVERLAP_DATASETS or NON_OVERLAP_DATASETS")
        dataset_names = [args.tabarena_name]
    overlap_by_name = {overlap.tabarena_name: overlap for overlap in OVERLAP_DATASETS}

    here = Path(__file__).parent
    run_name = "tabarena_leakage_analysis"
    new_result_prefix = "[Leakage] "
    results_dir = str(here / "experiments" / run_name)
    eval_dir = here / "eval" / run_name

    context = TabArenaContext()
    if not args.compare_only:
        for name in dataset_names:
            overlap = overlap_by_name.get(name)
            if overlap is not None:
                arms, generators = "base/correct/wrong", build_checkpoint_generators(overlap)
            else:
                arms, generators = f"base/correct(fold k{NON_OVERLAP_FOLD})", build_non_overlap_generators()
            print(f"\n=== Running TabSTAR {arms} on {name} ===")
            experiments = TabArenaV0pt1ExperimentBundle(
                models=[(gen, 0) for gen in generators],
            ).build_experiments()
            context.build_and_run_jobs(
                experiments,
                expname=results_dir,
                subset="lite",
                build_kwargs={"dataset_names": [name]},
                new_result_prefix=new_result_prefix,
                debug_mode=True,
            )

    if args.skip_compare:
        print(f"\nSkipping compare(); results persisted under {results_dir}")
    else:
        if args.compare_only:
            # compare() only sees methods that build_and_run_jobs() registered in this process,
            # so a fresh process has to re-ingest the persisted raw results first.
            from tabarena.end_to_end import EndToEnd

            end_to_end = EndToEnd.from_path_raw(path_raw=results_dir, cache=False, backend="native")
            context = TabArenaContext(
                extra_methods=end_to_end.to_method_metadata_lst(new_result_prefix=new_result_prefix),
                only_valid_tasks=True,
            )
        leaderboard = context.compare(output_dir=eval_dir)
        leaderboard_website = context.leaderboard_to_website_format(leaderboard=leaderboard)
        print("\n=== TabArena leaderboard (website format) ===")
        print(leaderboard_website.to_markdown(index=False))
        print(f"\nView saved figures in {eval_dir}")

# TabArena leakage analysis: results

Outputs of `../tabarena_leakage_analysis.py` on all 51 TabArena v0.1 datasets, split r0f0,
87 methods (TabArena's 84 plus the three arms below). Boards were scored with TabArena's
own scorer (`bencheval.BenchmarkEvaluator`, Elo calibrated to `RF (default)` = 1000,
200 bootstrap rounds).

Arms (raw method keys in the CSVs, and the names used in the page):

| Raw key | Name | Checkpoint |
|---|---|---|
| `[Leakage] TabSTAR_base (default)` | TabSTAR-Default-NoBug-WithLeakage-400 | released `alana89/TabSTAR`, pretrained on all 400 datasets |
| `[Leakage] TabSTAR_correct (default)` | TabSTAR-Default-NoBug-NoLeakage | the 320-dataset fold checkpoint that excluded the dataset (fold k0 off corpus) |
| `[Leakage] TabSTAR_wrong (default)` | TabSTAR-Default-NoBug-WithLeakage-320 | a 320-dataset fold checkpoint that still included the dataset (in corpus only) |

Files:

- `results_per_split.csv`: one row per method and dataset with the metric error, times and the
  `imputed` flag (TabArena fills the 320 arm's 19 off-corpus rows from `RF (default)` here).
- `tabarena_leaderboard.csv`: TabArena's leaderboard of the run, 87 methods, RF fill.
- `per_type_boards_impute_400.csv`: boards for all tasks, classification, binary, multiclass and
  regression, with the 320 arm's 19 off-corpus rows filled from the 400 arm instead of RF.
- `per_type_boards_drop320.csv`: the same boards without the 320 arm, 86 methods, no filled rows.
- `per_dataset_leakage.csv`: per dataset, the rank of each arm among the 87 methods on the split
  and the gain of each leaky arm over NoLeakage in places, in relative error and in units of the
  board's error spread. The 19 off-corpus datasets serve as the leak-free control.
- `tabstar_leakage_boards.html`: the sortable boards page built from the files above.

import psutil
from typing import Dict, Optional
import torch

from tabstar.training.devices import CPU_CORES


def get_hardware_dict(device: torch.device) -> Dict:
    return {**_get_gpu_dict(device), **_get_cpu_dict()}


def _get_gpu_dict(device: torch.device) -> Dict:
    use_gpu = False
    gpu_type = None
    gpu_total_mem_gb = None
    if device.type == 'cuda':
        use_gpu = True
        gpu_index = device.index if device.index is not None else torch.cuda.current_device()
        gpu_type = torch.cuda.get_device_name(gpu_index)
        gpu_total_mem = torch.cuda.get_device_properties(gpu_index).total_memory
        gpu_total_mem_gb = byte_to_gb(gpu_total_mem)
    return {"gpu_type": gpu_type, "gpu_total_memory_gb": gpu_total_mem_gb, "use_gpu": use_gpu}


def _get_cpu_dict() -> Dict:
    ram_bytes = psutil.virtual_memory().total
    ram_gb = byte_to_gb(ram_bytes)
    cpu_name = get_cpu_name_linux()
    return {"cpu_cores": CPU_CORES, "system_ram_gb": ram_gb, "cpu_name": cpu_name}


def byte_to_gb(byte_size: int) -> float:
    return byte_size / (1024 ** 3)


def get_cpu_name_linux() -> Optional[str]:
    try:
        with open("/proc/cpuinfo") as f:
            for line in f:
                if "model name" in line:
                    return line.split(":", 1)[1].strip()
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return None

FILE: tabstar_paper/utils/io_handlers.py

import json
import os
from json import JSONDecodeError
from typing import Dict, List


def load_json(path: str) -> Dict:
    with open(path, 'r') as file:
        try:
            data = json.load(file)
        except JSONDecodeError as e:
            print(f"Error in file {path}: {e}")
            raise e
    return data

def load_json_lines(path: str) -> List[Dict]:
    with open(path, 'r') as file:
        data = [json.loads(line) for line in file]
    return data

def dump_json(data: Dict, path: str) -> None:
    create_dir(path)
    with open(path, 'w') as file:
        json.dump(data, file, indent=4)

def dump_json_lines(data, path: str) -> None:
    with open(path, 'w') as file:
        for d in data:
            json.dump(d, file)
            file.write('\n')


def create_dir(path: str):
    dir_name = os.path.dirname(path)
    if dir_name:
        os.makedirs(dir_name, exist_ok=True)

FILE: tabstar_paper/utils/profiling.py

import os, time, threading, psutil
from typing import Optional
import torch

from tabstar_paper.utils.hardware import byte_to_gb


class PeakMemoryTracker:
    def __init__(self, phase: str, device: Optional[torch.device] = None, interval: float = 0.5):
        self.phase = phase
        self.device = device
        self.interval = interval
        self.max_rss = 0
        self.max_gpu_used = 0
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self.gpu_enabled = False
        if device is not None and device.type == 'cuda':
            try:
                import pynvml
                pynvml.nvmlInit()
                self.pynvml = pynvml
                self.handle = pynvml.nvmlDeviceGetHandleByIndex(device.index or 0)
                self.gpu_enabled = True
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Warning: NVML init failed, disabling GPU tracking: {e}")

    def _sample(self):
        proc = psutil.Process(os.getpid())
        while not self._stop.is_set():
            try:
                rss = proc.memory_info().rss
                self.max_rss = max(self.max_rss, rss)
            except psutil.NoSuchProcess:
                break
            if self.gpu_enabled:
                try:
                    info = self.pynvml.nvmlDeviceGetMemoryInfo(self.handle)
                    self.max_gpu_used = max(self.max_gpu_used, info.used)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            time.sleep(self.interval)

    def __enter__(self):
        self.start_time = time.time()
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._sample, daemon=True)
        self.thread.start()
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
        self._stop.set()
        self.thread.join(timeout=self.interval * 2)
        self.end_time = time.time()

    def summary(self):
        peak_ram_gb = byte_to_gb(self.max_rss)
        peak_gpu_ram_gb = byte_to_gb(self.max_gpu_used)
        return {
            f'{self.phase}_wall_time_s': self.end_time - self.start_time,
            f'{self.phase}_peak_cpu_gb': peak_ram_gb,
            f'{self.phase}_peak_gpu_gb': peak_gpu_ram_gb,
        }

# TabSTAR v1.1 pretraining

The code that produced the TabSTAR v1.1 checkpoints (`alana89/TabSTAR-v1.1-cls-20261011` and
`alana89/TabSTAR-v1.1-reg-20261009`). The model itself (architecture, preprocessing, finetuning) is
reused from the `tabstar` package under `tabstar.tabstarv1p1`; this folder adds what only
pretraining needs:

- `do_t4_eval_fingerprints.py`: fingerprints the evaluation tables T4 data must not contain.
- `do_t4_data.py`: builds a deduplicated, decontaminated pretraining pool from TabLib.
- `do_pretrain.py`: pretrains on that pool, scoring a finetunability Elo after every epoch.
- `do_finetunability_check.py`: the per-epoch finetune-and-score subprocess (called by `do_pretrain.py`).

## Setup

Requires Python 3.11+ and a CUDA GPU with bf16 support. After `source init.sh` (see the main
README), also install `tabarena[benchmark]` and `bencheval` from the
[TabArena repo](https://github.com/autogluon/tabarena); pretraining refuses to start without them.

Run every script from the repo root as a module:

```bash
python -m tabstarv1p1_pretrain.do_pretrain --help
```

No keys are stored in the code. Set them as environment variables, or copy
[`.env.example`](.env.example) to `.env` at the repo root (git-ignored) and fill it in:

| Variable | Effect |
|---|---|
| `WANDB_API_KEY`, `WANDB_ENTITY` | Log to WandB; without a key, WandB is disabled |
| `HF_TOKEN` | Hugging Face access for downloading TabLib/T4 chunks and the fingerprinted HF benchmarks |
| `KAGGLE_USERNAME`, `KAGGLE_KEY` | Kaggle access for `do_t4_eval_fingerprints.py` (MulTaBench tables) |
| `GPU` | Run on `cuda:<GPU>` |
| `CPU` | Force CPU (debugging only; pretraining itself needs a GPU) |
| `MONITOR`, `MEMORY` | Extra timing and GPU-memory logging |

## T4/TabLib data

TabLib is a large public corpus of tables scraped from GitHub. `do_t4_data.py` turns raw TabLib
chunks into dataset directories that pretraining can consume directly: it downloads and extracts
TabLib chunks (caching them under `--hf_cache_dir`), filters candidate columns as prediction
targets with a LightGBM-based learnability check, and writes one directory per accepted target
under `--output_dir`:

```
<output_dir>/cls/<name>/
<output_dir>/reg/<name>/
```

Each holds a `properties.json` and a `train/data.h5`, split by task type. `--d_num` controls the
numeric feature dimension. `--max_datasets` caps the total number of targets across both task
types; `--max_per_task` instead caps `cls` and `reg` independently. The two are mutually exclusive.

```bash
python -m tabstarv1p1_pretrain.do_t4_data --output_dir <path> --max_per_task 200000 --eval_fingerprints <fingerprints.json>
```

### Deduplication and decontamination

TabLib is full of copies, so tables are filtered before any target is curated:

- TabLib files are named `<hash>=__<uuid>.parquet`, and files sharing a hash are copies or
  resamples of one source table, so only the first file per hash is processed, across all chunks.
- Each remaining table is fingerprinted by content: every column with enough distinct values gets a
  MinHash sketch of its value set, which survives row subsets, row and column reordering, and
  column renames (see `pretraining/t4_overlap.py`).
- A table is dropped if it copies an evaluation table, meaning its column-name set equals that
  table's, or at least two of its columns match that table's columns by content.
- A table is dropped if at least two of its columns match an earlier kept T4 table by content.
  Column names alone never count here, since unrelated TabLib tables often share generic headers.
- Tables too small or too large to yield any target are never fingerprinted, so they never shadow
  a larger copy found later.

Every dropped table still gets a rejected verdict per column in the curation cache below, with
reason `duplicate_hash`, `eval_copy` or `near_duplicate` and the table it duplicates in
`duplicate_of`. These verdicts are recomputed on every run, so a new fingerprint file needs no new
`--curation_version`. Decisions follow sorted file order, so a resumed run keeps the same tables.

Blocking evaluation copies needs a fingerprint file of TabArena, BeyondArena, STRABLE and
MulTaBench, built once with:

```bash
python -m tabstarv1p1_pretrain.do_t4_eval_fingerprints --out <fingerprints.json>
```

It downloads every TabArena dataset from OpenML, every BeyondArena and STRABLE source table from
their HF repos (about 11 GB), and every MulTaBench `data.csv` from Kaggle. Without
`--eval_fingerprints`, `do_t4_data.py` still deduplicates but blocks no evaluation copies, and
warns loudly. Resuming keeps every dataset an `--output_dir` already holds, so a pool built under
different filtering needs a fresh `--output_dir`.

### Curation cache

The LightGBM accept/reject verdict per candidate column is the expensive step; verbalizing and
saving an accepted column is cheap. The two are cached separately:

```
<curation_root>/t4_curation_cls_<curation_version>/{accepted,rejected}/<name>/curation.json
<curation_root>/t4_curation_reg_<curation_version>/{accepted,rejected}/<name>/curation.json
```

Bump `--curation_version` to rerun the filtering itself; keep it fixed and point a new
`--output_dir` at it to re-materialize the same accepted columns under different preprocessing
(e.g. a new `--d_num`) without refitting LightGBM.

## Pretraining

`--t4_dir` points at an `--output_dir` produced by `do_t4_data.py`. `--task_type cls`/`reg`
(required) trains one model per task type. `--num_datasets` sets how many datasets each epoch
draws. `--pool_mode active` (default) resamples that subset from the full pool every epoch;
`random` samples it once at startup; `first` takes the first `--num_datasets` in sorted order.

After every epoch, the checkpoint is LoRA-finetuned on a capped subsample of TabArena's fold 0
for each dataset of its task type, and its scores are battled against the baselines in
`finetunability/baseline_scores.csv` (scored under the same protocol) into a finetunability Elo.
The released checkpoints are the epochs with the best finetunability Elo.

The v1.1 recipe, on a decontaminated pool of about 200K tables per task type:

```bash
python -m tabstarv1p1_pretrain.do_pretrain --exp cls50k-active --task_type cls --t4_dir <pool> \
    --num_datasets 50000 --pool_mode active --batch_size 512
python -m tabstarv1p1_pretrain.do_pretrain --exp reg50k-active --task_type reg --t4_dir <pool> \
    --num_datasets 50000 --pool_mode active --batch_size 512
```

Both ran 25 epochs; the released checkpoints are epoch 17 (cls) and epoch 16 (reg). Checkpoints
are saved under `../tabstar_pretrain_models/`.

import argparse
import resource
from dataclasses import asdict
from os.path import exists

import wandb

from tabstar.tabstarv1p1.training.devices import get_device

from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.arguments import REG_DIST_BINS
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.pretrain_args import PretrainArgs, PretrainConfig, TaskType, PoolMode, TEXT_BATCH_SIZE
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.finetunability.args import FinetunabilityArgs
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.pretrainer import TabSTARPretrainer
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.utils.devices import safe_gpu_training
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.utils.logging import git_info
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.constants import DEVICE
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.hyperparameters import (TEXTUAL_UNFREEZE_LAYERS, LR, WARMUP,
                                                       WEIGHT_DECAY, EPOCHS, PATIENCE, BATCH_SIZE,
                                                       TrainingArgs, MICRO_BATCH_SIZE)
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.utils.logging import wandb_run, wandb_finish


def raise_fd_limit() -> None:
    """DataLoader workers each hold an fd open per in-flight tensor (default `file_descriptor`
    sharing strategy); num_workers * prefetch_factor can exceed the default 1024 soft
    RLIMIT_NOFILE and deadlock the DataLoader on EMFILE instead of raising. Raise the soft
    limit to the hard ceiling before any workers are spawned.
    """
    soft, hard = resource.getrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_NOFILE)
    resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_NOFILE, (hard, hard))


def do_pretrain(train_args: TrainingArgs,
                pretrain_args: PretrainArgs,
                finetunability_args: FinetunabilityArgs,
                wandb_project: str = "tabstarv1p1_pretrain") -> str:
    if exists(pretrain_args.path):
        print(f"Pretraining model already exists for {pretrain_args.raw_exp_name}")
        return
    device = get_device(device=DEVICE)
    wandb_run(exp_name=pretrain_args.raw_exp_name, project=wandb_project, tags=[pretrain_args.task_type])
    d_summary = {**asdict(pretrain_args), **asdict(train_args), **asdict(finetunability_args),
                'full_exp_name': pretrain_args.full_exp_name}
    wandb.config.update(d_summary, allow_val_change=True)
    model = TabSTARPretrainer(run_name=pretrain_args.full_exp_name,
                              device=device,
                              train_args=train_args,
                              pretrain_args=pretrain_args,
                              finetunability_args=finetunability_args)
    print(f"ðŸ§ª [{git_info()}] Initializing exp {pretrain_args.full_exp_name} "
          f"over {model.num_t4_datasets_used} T4 datasets ({pretrain_args.task_type})")
    pretrain_args.num_datasets = model.num_t4_datasets_used
    pretrain_args.t4_pool_sizes = model.t4_pool_sizes
    wandb.config.update({'num_datasets': pretrain_args.num_datasets, 't4_pool_sizes': pretrain_args.t4_pool_sizes},
                        allow_val_change=True)
    PretrainConfig(pretrain_args=pretrain_args, train_args=train_args,
                  finetunability_args=finetunability_args).to_json()
    model.train()
    print(f"ðŸŒŸ TabSTAR was pretrained. The experiment name is: {pretrain_args.full_exp_name}")
    wandb_finish(d_summary)
    return pretrain_args.full_exp_name



if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise_fd_limit()

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run the training script with optional arguments.")
    # General
    parser.add_argument('--exp', type=str, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--wandb_project', type=str, default="tabstarv1p1_pretrain")
    # Arch
    parser.add_argument('--e5_unfreeze_layers', type=int, default=TEXTUAL_UNFREEZE_LAYERS)
    parser.add_argument('--text_batch_size', type=int, default=TEXT_BATCH_SIZE)
    # Data
    parser.add_argument('--t4_dir', type=str, default=None,
                        help="Dir of pre-built T4 RealDataset dirs (from do_t4_data.py) to add to pretraining.")
    parser.add_argument('--num_datasets', type=int, default=1000,
                        help="Cap on how many T4 datasets to sample from --t4_dir.")
    parser.add_argument('--task_type', choices=list(TaskType), required=True,
                        help="Restrict T4 datasets (and finetunability tracking) to one task type.")
    parser.add_argument('--pool_mode', choices=list(PoolMode), default=PoolMode.ACTIVE,
                        help="How --num_datasets get drawn from --t4_dir: 'random' samples once at "
                             "startup and keeps that subset for the whole run; 'active' resamples a "
                             "fresh random subset from the full pool at the start of every epoch; "
                             "'first' deterministically takes the first --num_datasets dirs in sorted "
                             "order and keeps that fixed subset for the whole run.")
    parser.add_argument('--reg_dist_bins', type=int, default=REG_DIST_BINS,
                        help="Number of bins for the regression head's distributional sub-head.")
    # Training
    parser.add_argument('--learning_rate', type=float, default=LR)
    parser.add_argument('--warmup', type=float, default=WARMUP)
    parser.add_argument('--weight_decay', type=float, default=WEIGHT_DECAY)
    parser.add_argument('--epochs', type=int, default=EPOCHS)
    parser.add_argument('--patience', type=int, default=PATIENCE)
    parser.add_argument('--micro_batch_size', type=int, default=MICRO_BATCH_SIZE)
    parser.add_argument('--batch_size', type=int, default=BATCH_SIZE)
    # Finetunability tracking
    parser.add_argument('--finetunability_timeout_s', dest='timeout_s', type=float, default=1800.0)
    parser.add_argument('--finetunability_skip_epoch_zero', dest='skip_epoch_zero',
                        action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction, default=True,
                        help="Skip the finetunability check before the first pretrain epoch.")

    args = parser.parse_args()
    train = TrainingArgs.from_args(args)
    pretrain = PretrainArgs.from_args(args=args)
    finetunability = FinetunabilityArgs.from_args(args)
    with safe_gpu_training():
        full_exp_name = do_pretrain(train_args=train, pretrain_args=pretrain,
                                    finetunability_args=finetunability, wandb_project=args.wandb_project)
    # Printing this for retrieval of automatic pipeline
    print(full_exp_name)

import os

# Must run before any other import: torch/numpy/lightgbm size their thread pools at
# library-load time, and `tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.hdf5` pulls in torch before
# `tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.t4_data` gets a chance to set these itself.
for _var in ("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ[_var] = "1"

import argparse

import tabstarv1p1_pretrain.constants  # noqa: F401, loads .env
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.hdf5 import RealDatasetProperties
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.t4_data import DEFAULT_HF_CACHE_DIR, DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR, create_t4_data
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.arguments import D_NUM

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Prepare T4/TabLib-derived pretrain datasets.")
    parser.add_argument('--hf_cache_dir', type=str, default=DEFAULT_HF_CACHE_DIR,
                        help="Directory holding the extracted (or still-zipped) chunk-XXXX T4 folders. "
                             "Downloaded here automatically if missing.")
    parser.add_argument('--output_dir', type=str, default=DEFAULT_OUTPUT_DIR,
                        help="Where prepared RealDataset dirs get written.")
    target_group = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    target_group.add_argument('--max_datasets', type=int, default=None,
                              help="Stop once this many datasets (cls + reg combined) are saved.")
    target_group.add_argument('--max_per_task', type=int, default=None,
                              help="Stop each task type independently once it reaches this many datasets, "
                                   "skipping further evaluation of a task type that already has enough.")
    parser.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument('--d_num', type=int, default=D_NUM)
    parser.add_argument('--curation_root', type=str, default="../t4_curation",
                        help="Parent dir for the versioned t4_curation_{cls,reg}_<curation_version> cache folders.")
    parser.add_argument('--curation_version', type=str, default="v1",
                        help="Cache is keyed by this; bump it to force re-running the LightGBM filtering.")
    parser.add_argument('--eval_fingerprints', type=str, default=None,
                        help="JSON from do_t4_eval_fingerprints.py; T4 tables copying one of its tables are dropped.")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.max_datasets is None and args.max_per_task is None:
        args.max_datasets = 10_000

    saved = create_t4_data(hf_cache_dir=args.hf_cache_dir, output_dir=args.output_dir, d_num=args.d_num,
                           max_datasets=args.max_datasets, max_per_task=args.max_per_task, workers=args.workers,
                           curation_root=args.curation_root, curation_version=args.curation_version,
                           eval_fingerprints=args.eval_fingerprints)
    n_cls = sum(RealDatasetProperties.from_json(d).is_cls for d in saved)
    n_reg = len(saved) - n_cls
    print(f"ðŸŒŸ Prepared {len(saved)} T4 datasets under {args.output_dir} ({n_reg} reg, {n_cls} cls)")

FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/do_t4_eval_fingerprints.py

"""Fingerprints the evaluation tables that T4 pretrain data must not contain.

TabArena tables come from OpenML in full, target included, since a scraped copy carries it too.
BeyondArena and STRABLE tables come from every source parquet in their HF repos, streamed because
some are several GB. MulTaBench tables are each dataset's Kaggle `data.csv`, images excluded. The
output JSON is what `do_t4_data.py --eval_fingerprints` blocks against.
"""
import argparse
import json
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

import tabstarv1p1_pretrain.constants  # noqa: F401, loads .env
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.t4_overlap import SKETCH_SIZE, frame_signatures, parquet_signatures

BEYONDARENA_REPO_ID = "TabArena/BeyondArena"
STRABLE_REPO_ID = "inria-soda/STRABLE-benchmark"
STRABLE_REVISION = "335c0a77595d4a9ddd86f46dc9c3b414f63a09a8"
MULTABENCH_KAGGLE_OWNER = "chico89"
# Core image, core text and full-text extra lists of alanarazi7/MulTaBench.
MULTABENCH_SLUGS = (
    "multabench-celeb-attractiveness", "multabench-hateful-meme", "multabench-mammography-cmmd",
    "multabench-chexpert", "multabench-cbis-ddsm", "multabench-glaucoma-smdg", "multabench-csgo-skin",
    "multabench-flower-bouquets", "multabench-hubmap-hpa", "multabench-justin-instagram", "multabench-petfinder",
    "multabench-zooscan-zooplankton", "multabench-amazon-bestseller", "multabench-amazon-packages",
    "multabench-hnm-fashion", "multabench-khaadi-clothes", "multabench-letterboxd-movies", "multabench-mango-mass",
    "multabench-mkphoto-bots", "multabench-painting-price",
    "multabench-fake-job-posting", "multabench-jigsaw-toxicity", "multabench-kickstarter-funding",
    "multabench-data-scientist-salary", "multabench-michelin-restaurants", "multabench-product-sentiment",
    "multabench-spotify-genres", "multabench-us-accidents", "multabench-wine-review",
    "multabench-women-clothing-review", "multabench-babies-prices", "multabench-book-price",
    "multabench-book-readability", "multabench-mercari-marketplace", "multabench-montgomery-salaries",
    "multabench-rotten-tomatoes", "multabench-scimagojr-impact", "multabench-vancouver-salaries",
    "multabench-video-games-sales", "multabench-zomato-restaurants",
    "multabench-full-wine-polish-market", "multabench-full-korean-drama", "multabench-full-used-car-saudi",
    "multabench-full-consumer-complaint", "multabench-full-wine-vivino-spain",
    "multabench-full-chocolate-bar-ratings", "multabench-full-anime-planet", "multabench-full-used-car-pakistan",
    "multabench-full-books-goodreads", "multabench-full-ramen-ratings", "multabench-full-osha-injury",
    "multabench-full-fifa22-wages", "multabench-full-california-prices", "multabench-full-wikiliq-prices",
    "multabench-full-american-eagle-prices", "multabench-full-airbnb-seattle", "multabench-full-news-channel",
    "multabench-full-imdb-genre", "multabench-full-melbourne-airbnb", "multabench-full-box-office",
)
FORMAT_VERSION = "t4-eval-fingerprints-v1"


def _record(table_id: str, source: str, schema: str, sketches: List[List[int]]) -> Dict:
    return {"table_id": table_id, "source": source, "schema": schema, "sketches": sketches}


def fingerprint_tabarena() -> List[Dict]:
    import openml
    from tabarena.benchmark.task.metadata.fetch_metadata import load_curated_task_metadata

    metadata = load_curated_task_metadata().drop_duplicates("dataset_id")
    tables = []
    for i, row in enumerate(metadata.itertuples(), start=1):
        dataset = openml.datasets.get_dataset(int(row.dataset_id), download_data=True)
        frame, _, _, _ = dataset.get_data(dataset_format="dataframe")
        schema, sketches = frame_signatures(frame)
        tables.append(_record(f"tabarena:{row.dataset_name}", "tabarena", schema, sketches))
        print(f"[{i}/{len(metadata)}] tabarena {row.dataset_name} ({len(sketches)} sketched columns)")
    return tables


def _fingerprint_hf_parquets(source: str, repo_id: str, filename: str, revision: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict]:
    from huggingface_hub import hf_hub_download, list_repo_files

    files = sorted(f for f in list_repo_files(repo_id, repo_type="dataset", revision=revision)
                   if f.endswith(f"/{filename}"))
    tables = []
    for i, remote in enumerate(files, start=1):
        path = hf_hub_download(repo_id, remote, repo_type="dataset", revision=revision)
        schema, sketches = parquet_signatures(path)
        table_name = remote.split("/")[0]
        tables.append(_record(f"{source}:{table_name}", source, schema, sketches))
        print(f"[{i}/{len(files)}] {source} {table_name} ({len(sketches)} sketched columns)")
    return tables


def fingerprint_beyondarena() -> List[Dict]:
    return _fingerprint_hf_parquets("beyondarena", BEYONDARENA_REPO_ID, "dataset.parquet")


def fingerprint_strable() -> List[Dict]:
    return _fingerprint_hf_parquets("strable", STRABLE_REPO_ID, "data.parquet", revision=STRABLE_REVISION)


def fingerprint_multabench() -> List[Dict]:
    import kagglehub
    import pandas as pd

    tables = []
    for i, slug in enumerate(MULTABENCH_SLUGS, start=1):
        path = kagglehub.dataset_download(f"{MULTABENCH_KAGGLE_OWNER}/{slug}", path="data.csv")
        schema, sketches = frame_signatures(pd.read_csv(path))
        tables.append(_record(f"multabench:{slug}", "multabench", schema, sketches))
        print(f"[{i}/{len(MULTABENCH_SLUGS)}] multabench {slug} ({len(sketches)} sketched columns)")
    return tables


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Fingerprint the evaluation benchmarks for T4 decontamination.")
    parser.add_argument('--out', type=str, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--skip_tabarena', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--skip_beyondarena', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--skip_strable', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--skip_multabench', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()

    tables = []
    if not args.skip_tabarena:
        tables.extend(fingerprint_tabarena())
    if not args.skip_beyondarena:
        tables.extend(fingerprint_beyondarena())
    if not args.skip_strable:
        tables.extend(fingerprint_strable())
    if not args.skip_multabench:
        tables.extend(fingerprint_multabench())
    payload = {"format_version": FORMAT_VERSION, "sketch_size": SKETCH_SIZE, "tables": tables}
    with open(args.out, "w") as f:
        json.dump(payload, f)
    print(f"ðŸŒŸ Fingerprinted {len(tables)} evaluation tables into {args.out}")

FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/baselines/lgbm.py

from typing import Optional

from lightgbm import LGBMClassifier, LGBMRegressor
from pandas import DataFrame, Series

from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.constants import SEED
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.baselines.abstract_model import TabularModel


class LightGBM(TabularModel):

    MODEL_NAME = "LightGBM ðŸ’¡"
    SHORT_NAME = "light"
    USE_VAL_SPLIT = True
    USE_MEDIAN_FILLING = False
    USE_CATEGORICAL_ENCODING = True
    USE_TEXT_EMBEDDINGS = True

    def __init__(self, *args, num_threads: Optional[int] = None, **kwargs):
        # LightGBM's own thread auto-detection reads hardware core count directly and calls
        # omp_set_num_threads() with it, overriding OMP_NUM_THREADS rather than respecting it.
        # Callers fanning out many fits across worker processes (e.g. t4_data.py) pass this
        # to cap threads per fit; left unset, default (use-all-cores) is unaffected.
        self.num_threads = num_threads
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)

    def initialize_model(self) -> LGBMRegressor | LGBMClassifier:
        model_cls = LGBMClassifier if self.is_cls else LGBMRegressor
        params = {"verbose": -1, "random_state": SEED}
        if self.num_threads is not None:
            params["num_threads"] = self.num_threads
        return model_cls(**params)

    def fit_model(self, x_train: DataFrame, y_train: Series, x_val: DataFrame, y_val: Series):
        self.model_.fit(x_train, y_train, eval_set=[(x_val, y_val)], categorical_feature=self.categorical_indices)

FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/baselines/preprocessing/categorical.py

from typing import Set, Dict

from pandas import Series, DataFrame
from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder

from tabstar.tabstarv1p1.preprocessing.nulls import MISSING_VALUE


def fit_categorical_encoders(x: DataFrame, categorical_features: Set[str]) -> Dict[str, LabelEncoder]:
    return {col: fit_encode_categorical(s=x[col]) for col in categorical_features}

def fit_encode_categorical(s: Series) -> LabelEncoder:
    encoder = LabelEncoder()
    train_values = set(s).union({MISSING_VALUE})
    encoder.fit(list(train_values))
    return encoder

def transform_categorical_features(x: DataFrame, categorical_encoders: Dict[str, LabelEncoder]) -> DataFrame:
    x = x.copy()
    for col, encoder in categorical_encoders.items():
        s = x[col].apply(lambda v: v if v in encoder.classes_ else MISSING_VALUE)
        s = encoder.transform(s).astype(int)
        x[col] = s
    return x


================================================
FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/baselines/preprocessing/feat_types.py
================================================
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Set

from pandas import DataFrame, Series

from tabstar.tabstarv1p1.preprocessing.nulls import get_valid_values


MIN_TEXT_UNIQUE_RATIO = 0.8
MIN_TEXT_UNIQUE_FREQUENCY = 100


@dataclass
class SemanticFeatureTypes:
    categorical_features: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    text_features: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)

def classify_semantic_features(x: DataFrame, numerical_features: Set[str]) -> SemanticFeatureTypes:
    string_feat_types = SemanticFeatureTypes()
    for col in x.columns:
        if col in numerical_features:
            continue
        if _is_text_feature(s=x[col]):
            string_feat_types.text_features.add(col)
        else:
            string_feat_types.categorical_features.add(col)
    return string_feat_types

def _is_text_feature(s: Series) -> bool:
    values = get_valid_values(s)
    if not values:
        return False
    n_unique = len(set(values))
    if n_unique >= MIN_TEXT_UNIQUE_FREQUENCY:
        return True
    unique_ratio = n_unique / len(values)
    return unique_ratio >= MIN_TEXT_UNIQUE_RATIO

FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/baselines/preprocessing/numerical.py

from typing import Set, Dict

from pandas import DataFrame


def fit_numerical_median(x: DataFrame, numerical_features: Set[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
    return {col: x[col].median() for col in numerical_features}

def transform_numerical_features(x: DataFrame, numerical_medians: Dict[str, float]) -> DataFrame:
    x = x.copy()
    for col, train_median in numerical_medians.items():
        x[col] = x[col].fillna(train_median)
    return x

FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/baselines/preprocessing/target.py

from typing import Optional

import numpy as np
from pandas import Series
from sklearn.preprocessing import LabelEncoder

from tabstar.tabstarv1p1.preprocessing.target import fit_cls_y, transform_cls_y


def fit_preprocess_y(y: Series, is_cls: bool) -> Optional[LabelEncoder]:
    if not is_cls:
        return None
    return fit_cls_y(y)


def transform_preprocess_y(y: Series | np.ndarray, scaler: Optional[LabelEncoder]) -> Series:
    if scaler is None:
        return y
    assert isinstance(scaler, LabelEncoder), f"Scaler must be LabelEncoder if not None, but got {type(scaler)}"
    y = y.copy()
    return transform_cls_y(y=y, encoder=scaler)



================================================
FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/baselines/preprocessing/text_embeddings.py
================================================
import os

from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
from sklearn.decomposition import PCA

from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.constants import SEED

os.environ["TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM"] = "false"  # Suppresses warning, avoids deadlock
from typing import Dict, Set, Optional, List

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from pandas import DataFrame
import torch

from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.arguments import TEXT_MODEL as E5_SMALL, D_MODEL

PCA_COMPONENTS = 30

class E5EmbeddingModel:

    def __init__(self):
        self.e5_model: Optional[SentenceTransformer] = None
        self.cached_embeddings: Dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}

    def load(self, device: torch.device):
        if self.e5_model is None:
            self.e5_model = SentenceTransformer(E5_SMALL, device=str(device))

    def embed(self, texts: List[str], device: torch.device) -> np.ndarray:
        self.encode_texts_in_batches(texts=texts, device=device)
        embeddings = np.array([self.cached_embeddings[text] for text in texts])
        assert embeddings.shape == (len(texts), D_MODEL), f"Got {embeddings.shape}, expected ({len(texts)}, {D_MODEL})"
        return embeddings

    def encode_texts_in_batches(self, texts: List[str], device: torch.device):
        self.load(device)
        new_texts = sorted(set(texts).difference(set(self.cached_embeddings)))
        embeddings = self.e5_model.encode(new_texts, normalize_embeddings=True)
        for text, embedding in zip(new_texts, embeddings):
            assert isinstance(embedding, np.ndarray)
            assert embedding.shape == (D_MODEL,), f"Got {embedding.shape}, expected ({D_MODEL},)"
            self.cached_embeddings[text] = embedding

    def clear_cache(self):
        self.cached_embeddings = {}


E5_CACHED_MODEL = E5EmbeddingModel()


def fit_text_encoders(x: DataFrame, text_features: Set[str], device: torch.device) -> Dict[str, PCA]:
    text_encoders = {}
    for col in text_features:
        # TODO: perhaps we should pre-compute all possible texts before the 'fit' call, for both train and test
        texts = x[col].astype(str).tolist()
        embeddings = E5_CACHED_MODEL.embed(texts=texts, device=device)
        pca_encoder = PCA(n_components=PCA_COMPONENTS, random_state=SEED)
        pca_encoder.fit(embeddings)
        text_encoders[str(col)] = pca_encoder
    return text_encoders


def transform_text_features(x: DataFrame, text_encoders: Dict[str, PCA], device: torch.device) -> DataFrame:
    for text_col, text_pca_encoder in text_encoders.items():
        texts = x[text_col].astype(str).tolist()
        embeddings = E5_CACHED_MODEL.embed(texts=texts, device=device)
        pca_cols = [f"{text_col}_pca_{i}" for i in range(text_pca_encoder.n_components)]
        pca_vec = text_pca_encoder.transform(embeddings)
        pca_df = pd.DataFrame(pca_vec, index=x.index, columns=pca_cols)
        cols_before = len(x.columns)
        x = x.drop(columns=[text_col])
        x = pd.concat([x, pca_df], axis=1)
        assert len(x.columns) == cols_before + PCA_COMPONENTS - 1
    return x

from enum import Enum


class SupervisedTask(Enum):

from argparse import Namespace

@dataclass
class FinetunabilityArgs:
    every_n_epochs: int = 1
    timeout_s: float = 1800.0
    skip_epoch_zero: bool = True

    @classmethod
    def from_args(cls, args: Namespace | Dict) -> Self:

        return init_dataclass(args=args, cls=cls, name="🏟️ Finetunability")

FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/finetunability/datasets.py

"""Picks all of TabArena's datasets for the finetunability signal, smallest-first by total cells
(rows x features) so scoring a checkpoint stays as cheap as possible.

Requires `tabarena` installed in this environment.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FinetunabilityDatasets:
    cls: List[str]
    reg: List[str]

    @property
    def all(self) -> List[str]:
        return [*self.cls, *self.reg]


def select_finetunability_datasets() -> FinetunabilityDatasets:
    """All TabArena classification and regression datasets, ranked smallest-first by total
    cells. Names match TabArena's own `dataset_name`.
    """
    from tabarena.contexts import TabArenaContext

    metadata = TabArenaContext().task_metadata
    total_cells = metadata["num_instances"] * metadata["n_features"]
    metadata = metadata.assign(total_cells=total_cells).sort_values("total_cells")
    cls = metadata.loc[metadata["is_classification"], "dataset_name"].tolist()
    reg = metadata.loc[~metadata["is_classification"], "dataset_name"].tolist()
    return FinetunabilityDatasets(cls=cls, reg=reg)



================================================
FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/finetunability/elo.py
================================================
"""Elo rating for the finetunability signal, battled against baseline models scored under the
same protocol (same fold, same train/test size, stored in `baseline_scores.csv`), not against a single anchor. Every
method present in `results` is rated together in one N-way battle; `bencheval.elo_utils.EloHelper`
already handles this natively once every method's scores are in one long-format table, so this
module is mostly that table's construction.

Both sides use TabArena's own per-dataset `eval_metric` (`roc_auc` for binary, `log_loss` for
multiclass, `rmse` for regression) â€” the metric the finetunability signal and the baseline scores in
`baseline_scores.csv` were computed with, so no separate scoring pass is needed here.
`metric_error()` turns that mix into one 'lower always wins' direction per row before the battle;
the three metrics are never combined into a shared scale, only compared dataset-by-dataset within
`EloHelper`'s own pairwise battles.

Uses the cheap, non-bootstrapped `compute_mle_elo`, appropriate for something meant to run every
epoch, not the bootstrapped, CI-bearing `compute_elo_ratings` a rarer periodic signal would afford.

Requires `bencheval` (from the TabArena repo) installed in this environment.
"""
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

import pandas as pd

CHECKPOINT_METHOD = "TabSTAR-v1.1-ckpt"


def metric_error(score: float, eval_metric: str) -> float:
    """`roc_auc` is higher-is-better and bounded above at 1; `log_loss` and `rmse` are already
    lower-is-better and unbounded, so they pass through unchanged.
    """
    if eval_metric == "roc_auc":
        return 1.0 - score
    if eval_metric in ("log_loss", "rmse"):
        return score
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported eval_metric: {eval_metric}")


def load_baseline_errors_csv(path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Loads a baseline-scores CSV (columns: method, dataset, score, eval_metric) into the
    long-format [method, dataset, metric_error] frame `compute_finetunability_elo` expects.
    """
    df = pd.read_csv(path)
    df["metric_error"] = df.apply(lambda row: metric_error(row["score"], row["eval_metric"]), axis=1)
    return df[["method", "dataset", "metric_error"]]


def compute_finetunability_elo(checkpoint_errors: Dict[str, float], baseline_errors: pd.DataFrame,
                               calibration_method: str, calibration_elo: float = 1000.0,
                               datasets: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """Elo rating for every method present, rated together in one battle.

    `checkpoint_errors` maps dataset name -> `metric_error` for the checkpoint being scored.
    `baseline_errors` is long-format: one row per (method, dataset) with columns `method`,
    `dataset`, `metric_error` (e.g. from `load_baseline_errors_csv`). A method only needs scores
    on the datasets it shares with others, not every dataset.

    `datasets`, if given, restricts the battle to that subset (e.g. classification-only or
    regression-only datasets) instead of everything both sides cover.

    `calibration_method` is held fixed at `calibration_elo` in every call, so ratings stay
    comparable across separate calls (e.g. one per checkpoint/epoch) even as every other score
    moves â€” pick a method with stable, already-trusted scores, not the checkpoint itself.
    """
    from bencheval.elo_utils import EloHelper

    checkpoint_rows = [{"method": CHECKPOINT_METHOD, "dataset": d, "metric_error": e}
                       for d, e in checkpoint_errors.items()]
    results_df = pd.concat([pd.DataFrame(checkpoint_rows),
                            baseline_errors[["method", "dataset", "metric_error"]]], ignore_index=True)

    helper = EloHelper(method_col="method", task_col="dataset", error_col="metric_error")
    battles = helper.convert_results_to_battles(results_df, datasets=datasets)
    elo = helper.compute_mle_elo(battles, calibration_framework=calibration_method, calibration_elo=calibration_elo)
    return elo.to_dict()

from argparse import Namespace
from dataclasses import fields
from typing import Dict, Any

from tabstar.tabstarv1p1.constants import INTERACTION_LAYERS, D_MODEL, D_NUM, TEXT_MODEL, REG_Z_CLIP, REG_DIST_BINS

# Data
DATASET_TRAIN_SIZE = 1024
DATASET_VAL_SIZE = 64


def init_dataclass(args: Namespace | Dict, cls: Any, name: str) -> Any:
    if isinstance(args, Namespace):
        args = vars(args)
    valid_keys = {f.name for f in fields(cls)}
    filtered_args = {k: v for k, v in args.items() if k in valid_keys}
    print(f"{name} args:")
    for k, v in filtered_args.items():
        print(f"  - {k}: {v}")
    return cls(**filtered_args)

from typing import List

from torch.utils.data import DataLoader

from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.hdf5 import RealDataset
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.training.dataloaders import get_mixed_dataloader
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.hyperparameters import TrainingArgs


def get_pretrain_multi_dataloader(data_dirs: List[str], train_args: TrainingArgs) -> DataLoader:
    datasets = []
    for d in data_dirs:
        dataset = RealDataset(data_dir=d)
        datasets.append(dataset)
    return get_mixed_dataloader(datasets=datasets, train_args=train_args)

FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/pretraining/hdf5.py

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, fields
from functools import lru_cache
from os.path import join
from typing import Self

import h5py
from torch.utils.data import Dataset

from tabstar.tabstarv1p1.preprocessing.verbalizer import TabSTARData
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.utils.tabular import task_type_from_d_output
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.datasets.objects import SupervisedTask
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.utils.io_handlers import load_json, dump_json


@lru_cache(maxsize=200)
def _open_h5_file(path: str) -> h5py.File:
    # A pretraining pool can hold far more files than a process's file-descriptor ulimit
    # allows to stay open at once, so the cache evicts the least-recently-used path; h5py
    # closes an evicted File as soon as this was its last reference.
    return h5py.File(path, 'r')


@dataclass
class RealDatasetProperties:
    name: str
    d_output: int
    train_size: int

    @classmethod
    def from_json(cls, data_dir: str) -> Self:
        properties_path = join(data_dir, RealDataset.PROPERTIES)
        d = load_json(properties_path)
        allowed_fields = {f.name for f in fields(cls)}
        d = {k: v for k, v in d.items() if k in allowed_fields}
        return cls(**d)

    @property
    def is_cls(self) -> bool:
        return self.d_output > 1

    @property
    def task_type(self) -> SupervisedTask:
        return task_type_from_d_output(self.d_output)


class RealDataset(Dataset):

    TRAIN = "train"
    X_TXT_KEY = "X_txt"
    X_NUM_KEY = "X_num"
    Y_KEY = "y"
    H5_FILE = "data.h5"
    PROPERTIES = "properties.json"

    def __init__(self, data_dir: str):
        self.file_path = join(data_dir, self.TRAIN, self.H5_FILE)
        self.properties: RealDatasetProperties = RealDatasetProperties.from_json(data_dir)
        self.size: int = self.properties.train_size

    @property
    def is_cls(self) -> bool:
        return self.properties.is_cls

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return self.size

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        h5_file = _open_h5_file(self.file_path)
        x_txt = h5_file[self.X_TXT_KEY][idx]
        x_num = h5_file[self.X_NUM_KEY][idx]
        y = h5_file[self.Y_KEY][idx]
        return x_num, x_txt, y, self.properties


def save_pretrain_dataset(data_dir: str, train_data: TabSTARData, properties: RealDatasetProperties):
    property_path = join(data_dir, RealDataset.PROPERTIES)
    dump_json(vars(properties), path=property_path)
    split_dir = join(data_dir, RealDataset.TRAIN)
    os.makedirs(split_dir, exist_ok=True)
    h5_file_path = join(split_dir, RealDataset.H5_FILE)
    key2data = {
        RealDataset.X_TXT_KEY: train_data.x_txt,
        RealDataset.X_NUM_KEY: train_data.x_num,
        RealDataset.Y_KEY: train_data.y
    }
    with h5py.File(h5_file_path, 'w') as h5f:
        for key, value in key2data.items():
            h5f.create_dataset(name=key, data=value)

FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/pretraining/monitoring.py
================================================
import subprocess
from typing import Dict, Optional

import torch

from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.utils.hardware import byte_to_gb


def print_gpu_mem_gb(device: torch.device):
    mem = gpu_mem_gb(device)
    if mem == 0:
        return ""
    else:
        return f"GPU {mem:.2f}GB"


def gpu_mem_gb(device: torch.device) -> float:
    if device.type != "cuda":
        return 0.0
    reserved = torch.cuda.memory_reserved(device.index)
    reserved = byte_to_gb(reserved)
    return reserved


def reset_peak_gpu_mem(device: torch.device):
    if device.type == "cuda":
        torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats(device.index)


def peak_gpu_mem_gb(device: torch.device) -> float:
    if device.type != "cuda":
        return 0.0
    return byte_to_gb(torch.cuda.max_memory_reserved(device.index))


def gpu_contention(device: torch.device) -> Optional[Dict[str, float]]:
    """Whole-device (every process on it, not just ours) utilization and memory for `device`'s
    GPU index, via one `nvidia-smi` query. A rough contention signal on a shared server: if
    `mem_used_gb` dwarfs our own `gpu_mem_gb`, something else is sharing the card. None if
    unavailable (no CUDA device, or `nvidia-smi` missing/failed).
    """
    if device.type != "cuda" or device.index is None:
        return None
    try:
        out = subprocess.run(
            ["nvidia-smi", f"--id={device.index}",
             "--query-gpu=utilization.gpu,memory.used,memory.total",
             "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10, check=True,
        )
        util_pct, mem_used_mb, mem_total_mb = (float(x) for x in out.stdout.strip().split(","))
        return {"util_pct": util_pct, "mem_used_gb": mem_used_mb / 1024, "mem_total_gb": mem_total_mb / 1024}
    except Exception:
        return None


================================================
FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/pretraining/optimizer.py
================================================
from torch import nn
from torch.optim import AdamW

from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.hyperparameters import TrainingArgs


def get_optimizer(model: nn.Module, args: TrainingArgs) -> AdamW:
    assert isinstance(model, nn.Module)
    biases, weights = [], []
    for name, p in model.named_parameters():
        if "bias" in name:
            biases.append(p)
        else:
            weights.append(p)
    lr = args.learning_rate
    param_groups = [{"params": weights, "weight_decay": args.weight_decay, "lr": lr, "name": "weights"},
                    {"params": biases, "weight_decay": 0.0, "lr": lr, "name": "biases"}]
    optimizer = AdamW(param_groups)
    return optimizer

from os.path import join

# Sibling of the checkout, shared by every worktree.
PRETRAIN_MODELS_DIR = "../tabstar_pretrain_models"


def pretrain_exp_dir(exp_name: str) -> str:
    return join(PRETRAIN_MODELS_DIR, exp_name)

def pretrain_args_path(exp_name: str) -> str:
    return join(pretrain_exp_dir(exp_name), "pretrain_args.json")


def get_pretrain_checkpoint(run_name: str, epoch: int) -> str:
    main_dir = pretrain_exp_dir(run_name)
    return join(main_dir, f"pretrain_checkpoint_{epoch}")

def get_finetunability_snapshot_path(run_name: str, epoch: int) -> str:
    """A transient per-epoch snapshot used to score that epoch's finetunability signal, then
    deleted â€” unlike the persisted training checkpoints.
    """
    main_dir = pretrain_exp_dir(run_name)
    return join(main_dir, f"finetunability_snapshot_{epoch}")

import argparse
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Dict, Optional, Self

from tabstar.tabstarv1p1.constants import TEXT_BATCH_SIZE
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.finetunability.args import FinetunabilityArgs
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.arguments import REG_DIST_BINS
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.paths import pretrain_args_path
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.hyperparameters import TrainingArgs
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.utils.io_handlers import dump_json
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.utils.timing import get_now


MIN_REG_DATASETS = 10_000


class TaskType(StrEnum):
    CLS = "cls"
    REG = "reg"


class PoolMode(StrEnum):
    RANDOM = "random"
    ACTIVE = "active"
    FIRST = "first"

@dataclass
class PretrainArgs:
    raw_exp_name: str
    unfreeze_layers: int
    timestamp: str
    num_datasets: int
    task_type: TaskType
    text_batch_size: int = TEXT_BATCH_SIZE
    t4_dir: Optional[str] = None
    pool_mode: PoolMode = PoolMode.ACTIVE
    t4_pool_sizes: Optional[Dict[str, int]] = None
    reg_dist_bins: int = REG_DIST_BINS

    def __post_init__(self):
        if self.task_type not in (TaskType.CLS, TaskType.REG):
            raise ValueError(f"task_type must be one of {list(TaskType)}, got {self.task_type!r}")
        if self.task_type == TaskType.REG and self.num_datasets < MIN_REG_DATASETS:
            raise ValueError(f"Regression pretraining requires --num_datasets >= {MIN_REG_DATASETS}, "
                             f"got {self.num_datasets}")

    @classmethod
    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> Self:
        return PretrainArgs(raw_exp_name=args.exp,
                            unfreeze_layers=args.e5_unfreeze_layers,
                            timestamp=get_now(),
                            text_batch_size=args.text_batch_size,
                            num_datasets=args.num_datasets,
                            t4_dir=args.t4_dir,
                            task_type=TaskType(args.task_type),
                            pool_mode=PoolMode(args.pool_mode),
                            reg_dist_bins=args.reg_dist_bins)

    @property
    def full_exp_name(self) -> str:
        return f"{self.timestamp}_{self.raw_exp_name}"

    @property
    def path(self) -> str:
        return pretrain_args_path(self.full_exp_name)


@dataclass
class PretrainConfig:
    """Full set of arguments behind a pretrain run, dumped for provenance. Nothing reads it back."""
    pretrain_args: PretrainArgs
    train_args: TrainingArgs
    finetunability_args: FinetunabilityArgs

    @property
    def path(self) -> str:
        return self.pretrain_args.path

    def to_json(self) -> None:
        dump_json(asdict(self), self.path)

================================================
FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/pretraining/t4_overlap.py
================================================
"""Content-based table matching, used to drop T4 near-duplicates and T4 copies of evaluation tables.

Each column with enough distinct values gets a bottom-k sketch: the k smallest keyed hashes of its
distinct values. Two sketches estimate the Jaccard similarity of the underlying value sets, so a
copy that keeps a subset of rows still scores in proportion to what it kept, and renaming columns
does not hide it. A table matches a reference table when their lowercased column-name sets are
identical, or when enough of its columns match columns of that one reference table.
"""
from collections import Counter
from hashlib import blake2b, sha256
import json
from math import ceil
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Tuple

from pandas import DataFrame

SKETCH_SIZE = 64
# Changing the key invalidates every stored sketch.
SKETCH_KEY = b"t4-overlap-v1"
# Low-cardinality columns (Yes/No, small category sets) hash alike across unrelated tables.
MIN_DISTINCT_VALUES = 25
# Below the ~0.5 a half-kept column scores, so row subsets still match.
COLUMN_MATCH_THRESHOLD = 0.30
MIN_MATCHING_COLUMNS = 2
# Bounds memory when streaming large reference tables; only the distinct-value floor reads it.
DISTINCT_CAP = 100_000

Sketch = List[int]


def _hash(value: str) -> int:
    digest = blake2b(value.encode("utf-8"), digest_size=8, key=SKETCH_KEY).digest()
    return int.from_bytes(digest, "big")


def schema_signature(columns: Iterable) -> str:
    names = sorted({str(c).strip().lower() for c in columns})
    return sha256(json.dumps(names).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _is_contiguous_int_range(n_distinct: int, lo: int, hi: int) -> bool:
    span = hi - lo + 1
    return span > 0 and n_distinct / span >= 0.9


def is_generic_integer_column(values: Iterable) -> bool:
    """Row indices, sequential ids and dense year columns match across unrelated tables."""
    integers = set()
    for value in values:
        try:
            integers.add(int(str(value).strip()))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return False
    if len(integers) < 2:
        return False
    return _is_contiguous_int_range(len(integers), lo=min(integers), hi=max(integers))


def column_sketch(values: Iterable, size: int = SKETCH_SIZE) -> Optional[Sketch]:
    """Bottom-k sketch of a column's distinct values, or None if the column is uninformative."""
    distinct = {str(v) for v in values}
    if len(distinct) < MIN_DISTINCT_VALUES or is_generic_integer_column(distinct):
        return None
    return sorted(_hash(v) for v in distinct)[:size]


def frame_signatures(frame: DataFrame) -> Tuple[str, List[Sketch]]:
    sketches = []
    for col in frame.columns:
        try:
            values = frame[col].dropna().astype(str).unique()
        except Exception:
            continue
        sketch = column_sketch(values)
        if sketch:
            sketches.append(sketch)
    return schema_signature(frame.columns), sketches


class _StreamingColumn:
    """Tracks one column's bottom-k sketch and integer range without holding its values."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.sketch: Sketch = []
        self.in_sketch = set()
        self.distinct = set()
        self.all_int = True
        self.lo: Optional[int] = None
        self.hi: Optional[int] = None

    def update(self, values: Iterable[str]):
        for value in values:
            if len(self.distinct) < DISTINCT_CAP:
                self.distinct.add(value)
            self._update_range(value)
            digest = _hash(value)
            if digest in self.in_sketch:
                continue
            if len(self.sketch) < SKETCH_SIZE:
                self._insert(digest)
            elif digest < self.sketch[-1]:
                self.in_sketch.discard(self.sketch.pop())
                self._insert(digest)

    def _insert(self, digest: int):
        self.sketch.append(digest)
        self.sketch.sort()
        self.in_sketch.add(digest)

    def _update_range(self, value: str):
        if not self.all_int:
            return
        try:
            number = int(value.strip())
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            self.all_int = False
            return
        self.lo = number if self.lo is None else min(self.lo, number)
        self.hi = number if self.hi is None else max(self.hi, number)

    def finalize(self) -> Optional[Sketch]:
        n_distinct = len(self.distinct)
        if n_distinct < MIN_DISTINCT_VALUES:
            return None
        if self.all_int and self.lo is not None and _is_contiguous_int_range(n_distinct, self.lo, self.hi):
            return None
        return self.sketch


def parquet_signatures(path: str) -> Tuple[str, List[Sketch]]:
    """Same signatures as `frame_signatures`, streamed so multi-GB reference tables fit in memory."""
    import pyarrow.parquet as pq

    reader = pq.ParquetFile(path)
    names = list(reader.schema.names)
    columns = {name: _StreamingColumn() for name in names}
    for batch in reader.iter_batches(batch_size=65536):
        frame = batch.to_pandas()
        for name in names:
            try:
                values = frame[name].dropna().astype(str).unique()
            except Exception:
                continue
            columns[name].update(values)
    sketches = [s for s in (c.finalize() for c in columns.values()) if s]
    return schema_signature(names), sketches


def sketch_jaccard(left: Sketch, right: Sketch) -> float:
    """Estimated Jaccard over the k smallest hashes of the union, valid when set sizes differ."""
    if not left or not right:
        return 0.0
    width = min(len(left), len(right))
    union = sorted(set(left) | set(right))[:width]
    shared = set(left) & set(right)
    return sum(1 for value in union if value in shared) / len(union)


class OverlapIndex:
    """Finds which indexed table a candidate table is a copy of."""

    def __init__(self, threshold: float = COLUMN_MATCH_THRESHOLD, min_columns: int = MIN_MATCHING_COLUMNS):
        self.threshold = threshold
        self.min_columns = min_columns
        self._schemas: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self._sketches: List[Tuple[str, Sketch]] = []
        # Similar sets share small hashes, so candidates come from an inverted index, not every pair.
        self._by_hash: Dict[int, set] = {}

    @property
    def size(self) -> int:
        return len(self._sketches)

    def add_table(self, table_id: str, schema: Optional[str], sketches: Iterable[Sketch]):
        if schema:
            self._schemas[schema] = table_id
        for sketch in sketches:
            if not sketch:
                continue
            position = len(self._sketches)
            self._sketches.append((table_id, list(sketch)))
            for value in sketch:
                self._by_hash.setdefault(value, set()).add(position)

    def match_columns(self, sketches: Iterable[Sketch]) -> Dict[str, int]:
        """How many of the candidate's columns match each indexed table."""
        hits: Dict[str, int] = {}
        for sketch in sketches:
            if not sketch:
                continue
            shared_counts: Counter = Counter()
            for value in sketch:
                shared_counts.update(self._by_hash.get(value, ()))
            best: Dict[str, float] = {}
            for position, shared in shared_counts.items():
                table_id, other = self._sketches[position]
                min_shared = ceil(self.threshold * min(len(sketch), len(other)))
                if shared < min_shared:
                    continue
                best[table_id] = max(best.get(table_id, 0.0), sketch_jaccard(sketch, other))
            # A candidate column vouches for a table at most once.
            for table_id, score in best.items():
                if score >= self.threshold:
                    hits[table_id] = hits.get(table_id, 0) + 1
        return hits

    def blocking_table(self, schema: Optional[str], sketches: List[Sketch]) -> Optional[Tuple[str, str]]:
        """The indexed table this candidate copies and the evidence, or None."""
        if schema and schema in self._schemas:
            return self._schemas[schema], "schema"
        hits = self.match_columns(sketches)
        if not hits:
            return None
        table_id, count = max(hits.items(), key=lambda item: (item[1], item[0]))
        if count >= self.min_columns:
            return table_id, f"content:{count}-columns"
        return None


def load_fingerprint_index(path: str) -> OverlapIndex:
    """Builds an index from a fingerprint JSON written by `do_t4_eval_fingerprints.py`."""
    with open(path) as f:
        payload = json.load(f)
    index = OverlapIndex()
    for table in payload["tables"]:
        index.add_table(table["table_id"], table["schema"], table["sketches"])
    return index



================================================
FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/pretraining/unfreezing.py
================================================
from typing import List

from transformers import BertModel

E5_SMALL_LAYERS = 12

def unfreeze_text_encoder(text_encoder: BertModel, layers_to_unfreeze: int):
    blocks = [block_name for block_name, _ in text_encoder.named_children()]
    assert blocks == ['embeddings', 'encoder', 'pooler'], f"Unexpected block structure: {blocks}"
    assert text_encoder.config.num_hidden_layers == E5_SMALL_LAYERS
    assert -1 <= layers_to_unfreeze <= E5_SMALL_LAYERS
    if layers_to_unfreeze == -1:
        freeze_the_whole_encoder(text_encoder=text_encoder)
    elif layers_to_unfreeze == 0:
        unfreeze_only_pooling(text_encoder=text_encoder)
    else:
        unfreeze_pooler_and_num_layers(text_encoder=text_encoder, num_layers=layers_to_unfreeze)
    total_params = sum(p.numel() for p in text_encoder.parameters())
    unfrozen_params = sum(p.numel() for p in text_encoder.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
    print(f"ðŸ¥¶ Unfroze {unfrozen_params:,} out of {total_params:,} parameters!")


def freeze_the_whole_encoder(text_encoder: BertModel):
    for name, param in text_encoder.named_parameters():
        param.requires_grad = False
    print(f"ðŸ¥¶ Freezing the whole text encoder - including the pooling!")

def unfreeze_only_pooling(text_encoder: BertModel):
    for name, param in text_encoder.named_parameters():
        if 'pooler' in name:
            param.requires_grad = True
        else:
            param.requires_grad = False
    print(f"ðŸ¥¶ Unfreezing only the pooling layer!")

def unfreeze_pooler_and_num_layers(text_encoder: BertModel, num_layers: int):
    for name, param in text_encoder.pooler.named_parameters():
        param.requires_grad = True
    for name, param in text_encoder.embeddings.named_parameters():
        param.requires_grad = False
    to_unfreeze = get_last_layers_num(to_unfreeze=num_layers)
    print(f"ðŸ¥¶ Planning to unfreeze {num_layers}/{E5_SMALL_LAYERS} layers: {to_unfreeze}!")
    layer_prefixes = [f'layer.{i}.' for i in to_unfreeze]
    for name, param in text_encoder.encoder.named_parameters():
        if any(name.startswith(prefix) for prefix in layer_prefixes):
            param.requires_grad = True
        else:
            param.requires_grad = False

def get_last_layers_num(to_unfreeze: int, total_layers: int = E5_SMALL_LAYERS) -> List[int]:
    layers_reversed = list(reversed(range(total_layers)))
    unfrozen = layers_reversed[:to_unfreeze]
    return unfrozen

================================================
FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/pretraining/data/dataloaders.py

from typing import List, Tuple

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import Tensor
from torch.nn.utils.rnn import pad_sequence

from tabstar.tabstarv1p1.preprocessing.tokenization import tokenize_x_txt
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.arguments import D_MODEL
from tabstarv1p1_pretrain.pretraining.hdf5 import RealDatasetProperties
from tabstar.tabstarv1p1.training.batching import TabSTARBatch


def pretrain_batch_collator(examples) -> TabSTARBatch:
    x_num, x_txt, y, properties = zip(*examples)
    x_num, feature_mask = consolidate_x_num(x_num)
    x_txt, tokenized = tokenize_x_txt(x_txt=x_txt)
    if all(isinstance(p, RealDatasetProperties) for p in properties):
        d_outputs = torch.tensor([p.d_output for p in properties], dtype=torch.long)
    elif all(isinstance(p, int) for p in properties):
        assert len(set(properties)) == 1
        d_outputs = torch.tensor(properties, dtype=torch.long)
    else:
        raise ValueError("Properties must be all RealDatasetProperties or all int!")
    y, is_cls = consolidate_y(y, d_outputs=d_outputs)
    batch = TabSTARBatch(x_num=x_num, x_txt=x_txt, tokenized=tokenized, y=y, d_outputs=d_outputs,
                          feature_mask=feature_mask, is_cls=is_cls)
    return batch

def consolidate_x_num(x_num: List[np.ndarray]) -> Tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
    seq_tensors = [torch.as_tensor(x, dtype=torch.bfloat16) for x in x_num]
    x_num = pad_sequence(seq_tensors, batch_first=True, padding_value=float("nan"))
    feature_mask = torch.isnan(x_num)
    assert feature_mask.dim() == 3
    feature_mask = feature_mask.any(dim=-1)
    assert feature_mask.dim() == 2
    x_num = torch.nan_to_num(x_num, nan=0.0)
    return x_num, feature_mask

def consolidate_y(y: List[np.ndarray | int], d_outputs: Tensor) -> Tuple[Tensor, bool]:
    if max(d_outputs) == 1:
        dtype = torch.bfloat16
        is_cls = False
    elif min(d_outputs) >= 2:
        dtype = torch.long
        is_cls = True
    else:
        raise NotImplementedError("We don't support mixed classification and regression batches!")
    y = torch.tensor(y)
    y = y.to(dtype=dtype)
    return y, is_cls


def consolidate_x_txt(x_txt: List[np.ndarray]) -> Tensor:
    assert all(arr.shape[1] == D_MODEL and arr.ndim == 2 for arr in x_txt)
    max_cols = max(arr.shape[0] for arr in x_txt)
    batch_size = len(x_txt)
    x_txt = [torch.tensor(arr, dtype=torch.bfloat16) for arr in x_txt]
    x_txt = pad_sequence(x_txt, batch_first=True, padding_value=0.0)
    assert x_txt.shape == (batch_size, max_cols, D_MODEL)
    return x_txt

FILE: tabstarv1p1_pretrain/training/dataloaders.py
